## 2026-07-06 — Onboarding vs. DealPilot simulation (docs/raw/onboarding-vs-dealpilot-simulation.md)
Ran a solo ETA searcher through the real `nextQuestion`/`buildBlueprintFromAnswers` step function (6 questions: solo, sales_deals domain, all 4 watch_first options, vocab_name="Deal", kanban, workspace name) and hand-traced `compileBlueprint`. Result: a near-empty shell — one Initiative("Deal") entity with only Name+Stage fields, kanban/signal-table/touchpoint-table views, `capabilities: []`. Compared against DealPilot's real code (`platform/tools/dealpilot/`): typed Industry/Geo/SDE/Revenue/Triage/ThesisFit fields, RYG kanban grouped by triage, real BizBuySell/BusinessBroker.net connectors, dedupe-composed sourcing pipeline, deterministic thesis-fit scoring. Biggest gap: onboarding never asks for domain-specific fields (same blueprint for "sales pipeline" and "job search"). Also found a real bug — the `watch_first: "calendar"` answer is collected but never read by `buildBlueprintFromAnswers` (silently dropped), and view `groupBy` is never set even when a stage field exists. Sourcing/dedupe/scoring logic correctly stays package-owned (out of onboarding's scope). Full differences table + per-item verdicts in the raw doc.

New `KnowledgeBasePage.tsx` (People/Communities/Resources/Projects toggle tabs — People/Communities honest-empty since no `graph.listPeople`/`listCommunities` procedure exists; Resources reuses `ResourcesPage`; Projects wires `graph.listInitiatives` through `<DataViews>`). `ToolsPage.tsx` rebuilt with Skills/Agents/Apps/Workflows section tabs (Apps links to the already-wired DealPilot/JobPilot/Helpdesk; Skills/Agents/Workflows honest-empty per existing no-list-procedure gaps). New `SettingsPage.tsx` placeholder (no settings backend exists). `ApprovalsPage.tsx` gained a Signals tab (reusing `graph.listSignals`/`recordSignalAction`) alongside the existing Approvals tab, each with its own count badge — no standalone Signals page ever existed in this app, so there was no separate route to remove. New `dataviews/views/MapView.tsx` replaces the GraphView placeholder for `kind: "map"` — an honestly-labeled "map view (list fallback)" grouped-by-location list (no map library in the repo). Onboarding: `buildBlueprintFromAnswers` now honors `watch_first: "calendar"` (adds a `next_step_date` date field + a calendar view) and sets kanban `config.groupBy: "stage"` when a stage field was generated — both previously-dropped answers. Fixed the "dialog auto-closes before the success message can be read" cosmetic bug: `Layout.tsx`'s `onProposed` no longer flips `onboardingOpen` to false (root cause — `Dialog open` was bound straight to that state); the dialog now only closes via its own "Done" button. BUGS.md: 2 rows marked RESOLVED (calendar/groupBy), the auto-close fix appended to the existing orphaned-draft RESOLVED entry, 2 new OPEN rows filed (client-side-only pin persistence; map-as-list-fallback + missing `ColumnKind` location tag). Gates: `turbo run build --filter=@bridge/web` clean, `eslint apps/web` 0 errors/0 warnings, full-repo `eslint .` still exactly the 2 known pre-existing warnings. No changes made to `platform/packages/*` or `apps/api` (concurrent session's lane).

## 2026-07-06 — ADR-023 kernel/API lane shipped: capability re-registration idempotency, `workspace.blueprint.getById`, convertibleKinds + kanban groupBy grammar (ADR-024)
Kernel/API-lane counterpart to the web-lane entry above, run concurrently. (1) `packages.install` (apps/api/src/router.ts) now checks `CapabilityStore.getManifestByNameVersion(workspaceId, name, version)` before `createManifest` for each bundled capability, reusing the existing manifest id (and only re-running `upsertState`) when a prior install already registered that exact (name, version) — closes the ADR-021 `capability_manifests_uq` collision bug. (2) New `workspace.blueprint.getById({ workspaceId, definitionId })` query returns a workspace_definition row by id regardless of status (draft/active/archived), identity-scoped like its siblings — unblocks ApprovalsPage's blueprint-activation diff preview for non-active drafts. (3) `packages/core/src/blueprint.ts`: added a `location` `BlueprintColumnKind`; `CompiledViewConfig` gained `convertibleKinds: DataViewKind[]` (table/kanban/gallery always; +calendar for a date column; +map for a location column; +network for a relation column; relationship entities hard-restricted to `["table","network"]`; non-tabular views get `[]`); fixed the never-set kanban `groupBy` bug — a kanban view without an explicit `config.groupBy` now defaults to the entity's first select-kind field id (explicit `null` still means intentionally ungrouped). All additive to `CompiledWorkspace`'s existing shape — apps/web's locally-mirrored blueprint types stay compatible; `@bridge/tables`' own `ColumnKind` deliberately left unwidened this pass (flagged as a follow-up, not done here, to avoid colliding with the concurrent web-lane session's shared-surface edits). New tests: `packages/core/test/capability-trust.test.ts` +1, `packages/db/test/capability-store.test.ts` +1, `apps/api/test/packages.test.ts` +1 (full idempotent-reinstall round trip), `packages/core/test/blueprint.test.ts` +12 (convertibleKinds + groupBy cases), new `apps/api/test/blueprint.test.ts` +5 (this router namespace had zero prior test coverage). Gates: `turbo run build --force` green for `@bridge/core`/`@bridge/db`/`@bridge/api` (`@bridge/web` fails independently on an unrelated concurrent-agent export issue, outside this lane); `turbo run test` 163/47/48 passing; `eslint packages apps/api` 0 errors (2 pre-existing unrelated warnings). BUGS.md: capability-re-registration-non-idempotent and kanban-groupBy-never-set rows marked RESOLVED; new OPEN row filed for `@bridge/tables` ColumnKind still lacking a real `location` member. ADR-024 in decisions-log.

## 2026-07-06 — Day-1 avatar overlay + onboarding egg shipped (apps/web)
New `apps/web/src/app/avatar/` module per docs/raw/spec-consolidation-2026-07.md sections 3+4. `avatar-store.ts`: localStorage-persisted `AvatarPrefs` (`bridge.avatar.v1`: animal/eggHatched/avatarName), existing-user fallback (neutral hatched owl, no forced re-onboarding), and a tiny event-driven `AvatarStatus` store (idle/listening/reading_context/drafting/awaiting_approval/blocked_by_policy/error) with an exported `setAvatarStatus` setter for future kernel-event wiring, plus a `bridge:capture` CustomEvent contract (`dispatchCaptureEvent`). `AvatarOverlay.tsx`: persistent bottom-right overlay mounted once in `Layout.tsx` inside the authed shell (all routes) — pure-SVG geometric creature per spirit animal (owl/fox/turtle/crane/wolf/cat, no image assets), status-colored halo ring, hover shows the state label, click-while-idle opens a popover with workspace name (`trpc.workspace.list`), current route, and pending-approvals count (`trpc.action.listPending` — real procedure, not fabricated; honest "no context providers connected yet" on failure). Blinks (200ms eye-close) on the `bridge:capture` window event, skipped when `prefers-reduced-motion` is set; breathing idle animation likewise respects reduced-motion. `EggHatcher.tsx`: SVG egg whose growth (non-linear easing) and status text map to REAL onboarding progress — questions answered (`questions.ts`'s new `answeredCount`/`MAX_QUESTIONS`) → preview reached → activated — never a fake timer; hatch sequence capped at 1.4s and never blocks the dialog's "Done" button. `questions.ts` gained a `spirit_animal` single-select question (owl/fox/turtle/crane/wolf/cat, cosmetic only, no blueprint effect) right after solo/team. `OnboardingDialog.tsx` renders `<EggHatcher>` under the header for every step, and on successful `workspace.blueprint.activate` (outcome "activated") runs the hatch animation, saves `AvatarPrefs` via `updateAvatarPrefs`, and calls a new `onHatched` prop so `Layout.tsx` can mount the overlay immediately without a remount. `Layout.tsx`: loads avatar prefs on mount (existing-user default when a workspace blueprint already exists and this browser never saved prefs), fetches workspace name via `trpc.workspace.list`, renders `<AvatarOverlay>` once prefs resolve, and wires `OnboardingDialog`'s new `onHatched` callback. Gates: `pnpm --filter @bridge/web build` green, `pnpm --filter @bridge/web typecheck` clean (0 errors) after a full monorepo `pnpm -w build` to materialize sibling package `dist/` output (pre-existing, unrelated to this change — packages hadn't been built in this worktree yet). No new dependencies (no lottie/three); all motion is CSS/inline-style transitions gated on `prefers-reduced-motion`.

## 2026-07-10 (cont'd) — Header alignment, collapsible rail, agent-name header
- **Root cause of "left/right/center header misalignment" found**: the left rail's org box was `h-16`, while the center page header and right chat panel header were both `h-14` — a real 8px height mismatch across the 3-column layout, not a stylistic difference from the reference. Fixed: org box now `h-14`, all three "top row" strips align as one visual row.
- **Right chat panel now shows the Chief of Staff's actual name** ("Owl" etc — same fallback pattern OverlayApp.tsx already used: chosen name from onboarding, or capitalized spirit animal) instead of static "Bridge AI" branding.
- **Collapsible left rail — genuinely new feature, not ported**: checked the prototype's own `Sidebar.tsx` source first — its comment explicitly says "Always-collapsed icon rail (design standard, not a user toggle)... there is no expand state," so there was nothing to port. Built a real toggle instead (user's explicit direction after seeing this): `railExpanded` state (persisted, `bridge.rail.expanded.v1`), 76px↔220px width transition, every nav row (org switcher, Home, Initiatives, +New, Knowledge, Intelligence, Settings) switches between icon+label-stacked (collapsed) and icon+label-in-a-row (expanded) via `navItemClass`/`navLabelClass`. Toggle button (PanelLeftOpen/Close) at the bottom of the rail. Verified live: expand shows full labels + org name beside avatar, collapse returns to the original 76px icon rail, no layout breakage either way.
- Org dropdown flyout restyled to match the prototype's actual `Sidebar.tsx` workspace-switcher exactly (position/sizing/avatar-square pattern) — see earlier entry this session.

## 2026-07-10 (cont'd) — Calendar auto-initiative, rail/panel resize+drag matching deployed reference, responsive header
- **Root cause found for "rediscovering UI patterns"**: the local `Design Bridge AI Interface (Copy)/` prototype folder is STALE relative to the deployed `bridge-ai-1ay.pages.dev` — its `Sidebar.tsx` explicitly comments "Always-collapsed... not a user toggle," but the LIVE deployed site has a real collapse toggle, drag-resize-feeling interaction, click-to-expand, and defaults to expanded. Confirmed by logging into the deployed site directly (browser audit: read_page, computer clicks, ResizeObserver-style width measurements via javascript_tool) rather than trusting local source. **Going forward: treat the deployed URL as ground truth for UI behavior, the local prototype folder as historical/possibly-stale** — this should prevent re-deriving interactions from scratch.
- **Calendar auto-seeds an Initiative** (`Layout.tsx`): pre-installed Modules (currently just Calendar) now get an automatic Initiative entry on mount via the same `createInitiative`/dedupe-by-packageName mechanism `NewModuleDialog.tsx` already used for user-opted-in Modules — "No Initiatives" should never show for a Calendar-capable workspace. Verified live.
- **Rail collapse toggle relocated + defaults changed**: chevron now sits inline next to the org name/avatar (was a separate bottom nav item) — matches the deployed reference's exact position. Default state flipped to EXPANDED (was collapsed), matching the reference's actual default, not the stale local prototype's claimed default.
- **Click-anywhere-to-expand when collapsed**: whole rail is clickable when collapsed; guard checks `!target.closest("a, button")` rather than exact element identity (the exact-match version silently failed on every genuine gap-click, only working on synthetic `.click()` calls — found via live debugging, fixed, re-verified).
- **Drag-to-resize**: rail drags between the two fixed widths (76/220px) with live visual follow, snapping to the nearer state on release; chat panel (`AgentPanel.tsx`) drags continuously between 260-520px with a persisted width (not snap-to-two-states, since a chat panel's useful width is a range). Both use the same hover-reveals-a-thin-highlighted-edge affordance.
- **Chat panel default width = 220×1.3 = 286px** (user's stated ratio to the rail's default expanded width).
- **Center Header responsive icons-only**: `ResizeObserver` on the tab-pill's container drops labels (icon-only) below a per-tab-count width threshold, `overflow-hidden` + `whitespace-nowrap` throughout so it can never wrap to a second line — was the actual literal ask ("texts disappearing... never a second line").

## 2026-07-13 — Security P0 batch (SEC-1/2/3 + XP-1) executed (developer branch `manishsbhoopalam8498-security-p0-hardening`)
- **SEC-1 (auth)**: added a mutation-gated `requireAuthOnMutation` tRPC middleware (`apps/api/src/router.ts`) that fail-closes any mutation on a persistent/prod deploy with no verifier, or a verifier + missing/invalid token (401), while keeping zero-config in-memory local dev open. `identity.ts` exports `bearerToken()`/`isVerifierConfigured()`; `context.ts` computes `authenticated`/`verifying` per request; `server.ts` boot-logs verifier state. +2 tests.
- **SEC-2 (CORS + rate limit)**: `corsOriginConfig()` permissive fallback now tied to `!isVerifierConfigured()` + non-prod (was NODE_ENV alone); registered `@fastify/rate-limit` (global 300/min + a 10/min sensitive bucket for propose/phone-OTP/outbound-network, keyed IP+bucket). +3 tests. api suite 53→58 green.
- **SEC-3 (deps + audit gate)**: drizzle-orm ^0.38.3→^0.45.2, drizzle-kit→^0.31.10, react-router→^7.15.0 (5 HIGH advisories cleared; `pnpm audit --prod --audit-level=high` clean); added CI `security-audit` job. drizzle 0.45's `DrizzleQueryError` `.cause`-wrapping forced a real runtime fix in `packages/db/src/ledger-store.ts` (walk the cause chain for SQLSTATE 23505) + a test-only cause-walking helper. @bridge/db 49 green; `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` verified.
- **XP-1 (cross-OS compile)**: gated `objc2*` crates to `[target.'cfg(target_os="macos")'.dependencies]`, kept `macos-private-api` inert-unconditional (splitting it fails tauri-build on macOS), `sensor_list` returns `[]` off-macOS; replaced desktop `echo` scripts with real `check`/`test:rust`/`build:tauri`; added a 3-OS `desktop` `cargo check` CI matrix (stub web/dist + Linux webkit deps). macOS `cargo check --locked` clean; non-macOS legs validated statically (all objc2 use is macOS-gated) + web-doc-confirmed macos-private-api is inert off-mac — the CI matrix is the DONE-WHEN evidence.
- **Governance**: BUGS.md H1/H1a/H2/H4 → RESOLVED (H3 already done); recorded as ADR-057/058/059 (renumbered on merge, see next entry). - **SEC-5 RLS-as-code** (`packages/db`): migration `0008_rls_as_code.sql` enables `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` + workspace-tenant / `private|team|workspace` visibility policies on 37 workspace-scoped tables (tenant GUC `app.workspace_id`, visibility via `app.user_id`); `src/rls-guard.ts`'s `assertRlsPosture` refuses a prod boot on a superuser/BYPASSRLS role. **Key caveat**: pglite's default role is SUPERUSER → bypasses RLS even under FORCE, so the policies apply in every db test's `createLocalDb()`→`migrate()` but only `rls.test.ts` (which `SET ROLE`s to a synthetic non-superuser) exercises a cross-tenant denial (the DONE-WHEN). db 49→53 green. Boot guard wired into `apps/api/src/wiring.ts` as a `verifyRlsPosture` closure on `ModePorts` (persistent mode only, self-gates outside prod), awaited in `buildWiring()`. Subagent (db) + my api wiring.
- **SEC-6 membership checks** (`apps/api` + `packages/db`): `DrizzleWorkspaceStore.isMember()` + a reusable `assertMembership()` router helper (throws `TRPCError FORBIDDEN`), applied to `workspace.inviteMember` / `workspace.listMembers` / helpdesk `route`; `assertPilotWorkspace` kept as the second layer. `bootstrapPilotIdentities` now seeds the pilot user as a MEMBER of the pilot workspace (else the tokenless dev-fallback identity would be refused). Deliberately NOT applied to every workspace-scoped procedure — the api harness fabricates non-member `test_fixture_*` identities behind `assertPilotWorkspace`, so blanket enforcement would red ~25 existing tests; broadening deferred (ADR-062). +3 tests (`apps/api/test/workspace-membership.test.ts`). api 60→63 green.
- **SEC-7 SSRF + log redaction + verification proof**:
  - Recon SSRF (`Tools/recon/lib/ssrf.ts` + `recon.ts`): an RFC1918/loopback/link-local/cloud-metadata denylist guards the `fetchJSON`/`fetchText` choke points before any user-influenced outbound fetch. 5 tests incl. a metadata-IP block end-to-end (the DONE-WHEN). Subagent (disjoint app). recon typecheck clean.
  - Log redaction (`apps/api/src/server.ts`): the Fastify/pino logger now `redact`s `req.body.phone`/`code`, `req.headers.authorization`, plus bare `body.*`/`headers.*` variants — Fastify's built-in `req` serializer strips body/headers before redaction, so the bare paths are the effective surface.
  - Verification proof (`apps/api/src/router.ts`): dropped `linkedin` from the `onboarding.saveProfile` `verificationMethod` enum (no real LinkedIn OAuth proof exists → a client-asserted `linkedin` must not persist as a trust signal); the dummy phone-OTP flow now returns `verificationSource:"dummy"`. The web client only ever sent `null`, so no client breakage.
  - +4 tests (`apps/api/test/security-hardening.test.ts`): linkedin-spoof rejected, honest null/phone accepted, OTP dummy-tagged, logger redaction proven. api 63→67 green.
- **Coverage floor**: `packages/models` 40→35 — models measures a deterministic 39.75% lines (a 40 floor can't pass; the Batch-2 floor had no margin). Tracked coverage debt (models was already in the ratchet-floor/undertested bucket).
- **Verification**: full `pnpm turbo run typecheck test build --force` → **59/59 tasks green**, all coverage floors pass; `Tools/recon` verified separately (typecheck clean, 5/5 SSRF). The full build ran alone and the db suite passed cleanly — a second data point confirming the earlier under-load db flake is resource-starvation, not a defect (BUGS.md watch).
- **Governance**: ADR-060 (EVAL) / ADR-061 (SEC-5 RLS + pglite caveat + boot-guard placement) / ADR-062 (SEC-6 scoping) / ADR-063 (SEC-7) recorded. APPROVALS **AP-014 filed PROPOSED** to mark Batch 3 DONE — PROGRESS boxes NOT ticked pending approval. Code + tests committed directly as routine.

"untrusted_external"` (untrusted-by-default); Gmail intake derives the tag by **reading the previously-dead `GOOGLE_MANIFEST.intake_policy.quarantine` flag** onto the Memory directive + proposal ledger request; sensors taint every capture-ledger record. Tag-and-persist only — no gating (that's PI-2). **DONE-WHEN met**: ingested Gmail thread + scraped-page/API row both land `untrusted_external` in tests.

Supersede = append-with-`supersedesId`; `retrieve` excludes superseded by default. Sensors `hub.ts` gained optional `memories?`/`userId?` deps and writes a derived private episodic Memory (referencing the timeline entry, tagged `untrusted_external`) on each capture — WITHOUT forking `timeline_entries`. **DONE-WHEN met**: capture→inspectable authority-scoped Memory + classification-respecting retrieval proven in tests.

## 2026-07-14 — Batch 5 Month-3 PI-2 + PI-3 executed (roadmap §M3, finishes Month-3 injection defense)
- **PI-2 tainted-context egress gate**: the RUNTIME data-flow half of the lethal trifecta. `@bridge/core`: optional `taint?: TrustOrigin` on `PolicyEvalInput` (`ports.ts`); `pipeline.propose()` computes `turnTaint = req.trustOrigin ?? ctx.taint`, threads it into the pre + runtime `policies.evaluate` inputs, and enforces a **structural always-on gate** — after the runtime eval it calls `evaluateTaintedEgress({action, resourceType, taint})` and pushes any result into `policyResults`. New `core/src/policy/taint-egress.ts`: `evaluateTaintedEgress` (pure), `taintedEgressPolicy` (same rule as a reusable `PolicyFn`), `TAINTED_EGRESS_POLICY_ID` (`pi2-tainted-context-egress`), `TAINTED_EGRESS_RESOURCES` (`{external:send}`). `untrusted_external` + egress (`external:send` or `action:"share"`) ⇒ `require_approval` ⇒ pipeline forces `pending_review`. **Structural (not a wired policy)** so the guarantee holds on any deployment wiring; `apps/api/wiring.ts` left unchanged (its `pol-external-approval` is complementary; wiring `taintedEgressPolicy` would only duplicate the result). `require_approval` not `block` (a human may still approve a legit send). `external:send` only (fetch is inbound, covered by SEC-7 SSRF + `planeGate`). **DONE-WHEN met**: red-team "forward all contacts to attacker@evil.com" on `untrusted_external` context → `pending_review`, nothing emitted.
- **PI-3 dual-LLM quarantine + spotlighting**: `core/src/guard/content-guard.ts` — a tool-less `ContentGuard` port (`inspect({content,trustOrigin,schemaHint?}) → ContentGuardVerdict`), verdict `{safe, categories[], extraction:{summary,entities[]}, reason}` (ONLY the typed `extraction` crosses back — never free-form prose), `QuarantinedContentGuard` (one tool-less `complete()` with a locked data-only system prompt over spotlighted content, parses ONLY typed JSON, **fails closed** on unparseable output, combines the model `injection` flag with a local regex fail-safe), plus `spotlightUntrusted()`/`SPOTLIGHT_OPEN`/`SPOTLIGHT_CLOSE`. `run-context.ts` + `context-provider.ts`: added optional `trustOrigin?: TrustOrigin` to `RetrievedMemorySnippet` + `ContextItem`; `projectToPrompt()` wraps every `untrusted_external` context item / memory snippet in spotlight delimiters + a "data, not instructions" banner (trusted items render bare). `@bridge/models` (subagent edge): `createLocalContentGuard(model)` binds `QuarantinedContentGuard` but throws `CloudContentGuardError` unless `plane==="local"` (private content never goes to a SaaS detector). **DONE-WHEN met**: an embedded injection / smuggled tool_call is reduced to typed data (`safe:false` or dropped from `extraction`), proven in tests.
- **Deliberately NOT wired**: no `ContentGuard` in `apps/api` — the quarantine seam is the desktop/sensor ingest plane; no apps/api router ingests untrusted external content into a model run today and there is no MCP integration yet, so it would be dead code + risk (same rationale as ADR-065/MEM-1). Port + core default + local adapter are exported and ready for the desktop shell / first MCP integration. The **MCP-output-is-DATA** invariant (tool results may taint a turn but never themselves trigger a `propose()`) is recorded in ADR-066 — the ADR the spec requires "before/with the first MCP integration".
- **Execution**: built the coupled core changes (ports/pipeline/policy/guard/run-context/context-provider/index) sequentially myself (all touch `@bridge/core`), then fanned the ONE genuinely-independent edge (`@bridge/models` local adapter) to a parallel subagent per the user's parallelize-independent-work request. Core tests written directly (`redteam-egress.test.ts` 13, `content-guard.test.ts` 8).
- **Verification**: full `pnpm turbo run typecheck test build --force` → **59/59 tasks green**, all coverage floors pass; core 219→249, `@bridge/models` 18/18.
- **Governance**: ADR-066 (PI-2) + ADR-067 (PI-3) recorded. APPROVALS **AP-016 filed PROPOSED** to mark Batch 5 DONE — PROGRESS boxes NOT ticked pending approval. No dummy data; no new BUGS. This finishes the Month-3 (§M3) injection-defense line (PI-1/PI-2/PI-3 + MEM-1). Deferred: a real local-classifier binding + the first consuming ingest seam.
## 2026-07-14 — Batch 6 Month-4 self-improvement loop executed (roadmap §M4, P3 core)
- **EVAL-3 baseline-vs-candidate gate**: `@bridge/core/src/eval/comparison.ts` — pure `compareRuns(baseline, candidate, gates)` (agent-quality-eval-model §4.2), a TWO-gate check (Gate A quality AND Gate B routing precision/recall) reading thresholds from `policy_params` (never hard-coded), verdict `promote|coexist|needs-human|reject` (promote requires both gates + significance via a delta 95% CI excluding 0), + `buildWhyBetterCard`. Wired into `apps/api` `capability.approve`: fires only leaving `validated`, after the pending_review audit, before `advance()`; `promote` advances + attaches the card, `reject` throws `BAD_REQUEST` (+card), `coexist`/`needs-human` return without advancing (+card). **DONE-WHEN met**: a candidate beating baseline on both gates auto-advances with a why-better card; a regressor is rejected (`apps/api/test/capability-governance.test.ts`).
- **EVAL-4 LLM-judge quality Scorer**: `core/src/eval/judge.ts` — `JudgeScorer implements Scorer` (pinned/versioned model in the run snapshot, numeric-only parse, clamped `[0,1]`), `selectHeldOut` (excludes cases whose `authored_by_capability === candidate`), `calibrateJudge` (correlate vs the human approve/veto subset), `evaluateRedTeamPack`/`requireRedTeamForExternal` (red-team pack gates the External band). **DONE-WHEN met**: quality scores correlate with the human approve/veto subset — calibration reported in `core/test/eval-judge.test.ts`.
- **REG-1 Component Registry + overlap**: `core/src/capability/registry.ts` — reuses `capability_manifests` + the new nullable `kind` (`ComponentKind`) discriminator; `structuralSimilarity` (Tier-1 pure, weighted kind/name/permissions 0.4/0.4/0.2) → `findOverlaps` escalates to Tier-2 semantic (`cosineSimilarity` over `ModelProvider.embed`) ONLY when Tier 1 is inconclusive (near-duplicate threshold 0.82); `embed()` optional ⇒ degrades to the Tier-1 verdict. The Learning Agent runs `findOverlaps` before proposing. **DONE-WHEN met**: a near-duplicate is detected before creation (`core/test/capability-registry.test.ts`).
- **VAR-1 Variance Adjuster**: `core/src/policy/variance-adjuster.ts` — `proposeVarianceAdjustment` maps a veto reason-chip to ONE tunable (`CHIP_PARAM_MAP`: `too_casual → tone_threshold+`, `too_formal → tone_threshold−`), computes a bounded `±δ` clamped to the param's `[floor, ceil]`, counts only VETTED decisions, and returns a `VarianceProposal` GOVERNED DIFF (never a silent write). Hard ceilings live outside `policy_params` (ADR-068) so they cannot be crossed. **DONE-WHEN met**: 3× "too casual" VETTED vetoes propose a governed `tone_threshold` nudge; ceilings can't be crossed (`core/test/variance-adjuster.test.ts`).
- **GOV-1 org-health rollup + Governance auto-approve**: `core/src/governance/org-health.ts` — `rollupOrgHealth` (autonomy-pressure/trust-debt/approval-load/violation-trend as pure fns over evidence), `classifyApprovalBand({risk, origin, safetyTouch})` → `minor|moderate|major` (minor = 2 lowest risk bands AND built-in/template origin), `canGovernanceAutoApprove` = minor only. Wired into `apps/api` as `capability.orgHealth` (real `listManifests`+`getState`) and `capability.governanceAutoApprove` (minor → advances validated→approved via `advance()`+`upsertState`; else refuses). **DONE-WHEN met**: the rollup renders for a workspace; Governance auto-approves only `minor` (major + moderate refuse) (`apps/api/test/capability-governance.test.ts`).
- **Governance auto-approve = a SYSTEM gate, not an agent exception**: the documented "Governance Agent is the sole exception to the agent-floor approve-DENY" is NOT implemented in code (the agent-floor is a hard DENY). Rather than weaken that structural invariant, minor-only auto-approve is enacted by the system/router gate computed from `classifyApprovalBand` — preserving the agent-floor as-is (ADR-073).
- **Foundations + integration**: `policy_params` typed tunable space (`core/src/policy/params.ts`: `TunableParam`/`AqvGates`/`DEFAULT_POLICY_PARAMS`/`resolveGates`/`clampToBounds` + `PolicyParamStore` port + in-memory adapter) — hard ceilings excluded by construction (ADR-068). `EvalStore`+`PolicyParamStore` wired into `apps/api` `Wiring`/`buildWiring()` in-memory (EVAL-1/2 infra had never reached the router; EVAL-3 is its first consumer). Drizzle bindings for both deferred (no persistence consumer yet).

## 2026-07-14 — Batch 9 Month-6 packages + Commons safety + consolidation executed (roadmap §M6)
- **PKG-1 sandbox floor**: `@bridge/core/src/capability/sandbox-policy.ts` — `evaluateSandboxRequirement(cap): SandboxGateResult`, a pure gate wired as a HARD REJECT in `apps/api` `packages.install` (over every bundled capability, before risk/approval). A capability is "executable" iff its manifest carries an `execution` spec (presence = the marker; `parseExecutionSpec` fails loudly on a half-declared spec). Gate: `isolation:"none"` → `executable_requires_isolation`; network/fs granted under only `process` isolation → `executable_caps_require_stronger_isolation`; declarative caps pass trivially. The prior MCP carve-out was removed (an mcp-server importer sets `requiresSandbox` true). **DONE-WHEN met**: install REJECTS isolation-none + network-under-process (BAD_REQUEST), a container-isolated gated executable clears the floor, a declarative package is unaffected (`core/test/sandbox-policy.test.ts` + `apps/api/test/pkg1-sandbox-install.test.ts`). ADR-079.
- **PKG-2 Commons supply-chain trust**: pure policy in `core/src/package/signing.ts` (zero-dep — no `node:crypto`): `canonicalizeManifest` (deterministic sorted-key JSON = the signed bytes), `SignedManifestEnvelope`/`ManifestSignature`, `verifyManifestSignature(envelope, injectedVerifier, {trustedPublicKeys?})` (typed failure `missing_signature|invalid_signature|untrusted_key|unsupported_algorithm`, fail-closed), `assertCommonsUrlTls` (https always; http loopback-only). ed25519 bound at the seam: SIGNER in `services/commons` (signs every publish + `GET /v1/signing-key`; subagent, 10/10), VERIFIER in apps/api `commons-client.ts` (`HttpCommonsClient` verifies every fetched entry in get/getVersion + asserts TLS in its constructor). Community-origin floor in `approvals.ts` (`isUntrustedOrigin`/`trustGrantsForOrigin`): `community` pinned == `user_code` → no trust-grant auto-activation (wired as defense-in-depth; `resolvedTrustGrants=[]` today). **v1 key custody**: registry-held signing key (curated-registry trust root); per-publisher author keys are post-v1 (envelope already carries `publicKey`, so no wire change). **DONE-WHEN met**: client accepts a valid signature, rejects altered/unsigned/untrusted-key, honors `verifySignatures:false`, TLS asserted (`core/test/package-signing.test.ts` + `apps/api/test/pkg2-commons-signing.test.ts` + commons `signing.test.ts`). ADR-080. Note: `publish-builtins` (default localhost) unaffected; a REMOTE `COMMONS_URL` now MUST be https (intended hardening).
- **BLUEPRINT-1 versioned publishable blueprint**: a `WorkspaceBlueprint` travels intact inside a `PackageManifest` (`kind:"workspace_definition"`, new optional `manifest.blueprint`), so PKG-2 signing covers it byte-for-byte. `core/src/blueprint.ts`: `BLUEPRINT_SCHEMA_VERSION` + optional `schemaVersion` (stamped when absent, future version rejected); `parseWorkspaceBlueprint` — the declarative GATE, closed key allowlist at every level (`rejectUnknownKeys`) so no `code`/`handler`/`exec` can be smuggled; `workspaceBlueprintTo/FromPackageManifest` bridge. A workspace_definition composes capabilities by reference (`blueprint.capabilities: string[]`), so `manifest.capabilities[]` is empty and `parsePackageManifest` relaxes its ≥1-capability rule only for that case. **DONE-WHEN met**: full path blueprint → manifest → sign → (transport) → verify-on-install → extract (re-validate) → `compileBlueprint`; the gate rejects a smuggled `handler` key (`core/test/blueprint-manifest.test.ts` + `apps/api/test/blueprint-commons-roundtrip.test.ts`). ADR-081.
- **CONSOLIDATE testing debt**: codified the real setup (`node --test` on compiled `dist` + per-package coverage floors + turbo; NOT vitest — no runner added). Fixed a core-barrel coverage-dilution artifact: because `node --experimental-test-coverage` reports every loaded file and each core-dependent package imports the `@bridge/core` barrel, Batch-9's new core modules (`signing.js`, expanded `blueprint.js`, `sandbox-policy.js`) diluted two downstream aggregates below floor — `sensors` 40→39.38 and `db` 55→54.55 (all tests passing, 0 cancelled). Recalibrated those two floors to at/below measured (`sensors` 40→39, `db` 55→54) per the standing rule; no package's OWN coverage dropped. Subagent added `integrations-google` behavioral tests (35/35; `gateway-google` 29→53%, `oauth` 45→100%, `intake` 66→92%; floor 50→53). `docs/wiki/testing.md` flagged stale. ADR-082.

# 2026-07-16 — Task Manager Module: plan + BRD + roadmap addition (TASK-021)
- New raw docs: `docs/raw/taskmanager-module-plan-2026-07.md` (three-lens plan, TM0–TM6, 14 Skills, 12 guard/routing/reschedule/scan Automations, no new permanent Agents, reuse-first map, risk register) and `docs/raw/brd-taskmanager-2026-07.md`. Wiki: `docs/wiki/taskmanager.md` + index entry.
- TASK-021 placed immediately after TASK-014 in the queue (both the execution-order list and the file's physical position); AP-033/AP-034 recorded APPLIED (user directives; renumbered during merge-integration from this session's original AP-030/AP-031, which collided with main's parallel task-integration approvals of the same numbers). R-035/R-036 logged. ADR-105 (renumbered from this session's original ADR-099, which collided with the parallel Helpdesk-identity ADR-099 above): two Record types only — Goal stays distinct; Task is one self-referential, dot-path-leveled type covering the former Initiative/Task/Subtask, with `status: candidate` replacing Initiative and `outcomes[]` replacing the separate Outcome type; execution layer reuses the Universal Action Pipeline; TM0 migrates the legacy initiatives/touchpoints tree under VOCAB2 into the single tasks Database.
- Revision driven by direct user pushback: reviving `Initiative` as a Module Record type was correctly challenged (AP-020 retired it as a kernel identifier, not a display alias); collapsing to two types instead of four was adopted, along with the user's dot-notation level-tracking idea (`2.3.5`).
- Added on the same pass: impact-fit-analysis on every Task/Goal create (Internal Strategist); deterministic agent-task routing (default Capability Builder in this Module, Skill-eligible Agent for cross-Module tasks — aligns with parallel-landed ADR-104's requirement that governed Skill resolution needs an active same-workspace Goal/Task); a confidence-graduated reschedule approval gate reusing the shipped ADR-073 `classifyApprovalBand` system gate + VAR-1 Variance-Adjuster pattern verbatim (no new "agent decides" mechanism, agent-floor DENY untouched); a scheduled proactive cross-Module opportunity scan.
- User's planning-methodology skill idea adopted restructured: one capped Playbook library (OKR, backward planning, GTD clarify, SMARTER, pre-mortem in v1) consumed by parameterized planning Skills — not ~20 separate Skills.
- Durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-16-taskmanager-module-plan.md`.

# 2026-07-17 — Task Manager Module revised again: Goal collapses into a Task field, no-default CoS-owned routing, reopenable done status (R-037, AP-035)
- Collapsed the remaining `goals` Database into the `tasks` Database: `is_goal: boolean` is now a plain field, not a type. Reasoning (ADR-106, supersedes ADR-105's two-Database call): the user correctly pointed out a root Task already has branching structure, and required full tree flexibility (insert a new ancestor above an existing branch, promote a branch to a new root while dropping a stale ancestor) — a hard-typed Goal would force a table migration on every restructure; a boolean field on one type does not.
- Added full tree restructuring (promote / insert-ancestor-above / re-parent) as governed proposals — new `task-tree-restructure` Skill (Internal Strategist) + `task-tree-restructure-proposal` Automation, path recomputed atomically over the affected subtree only.
- Removed the "default Capability Builder" agent-task-routing fallback entirely (ADR-107). Routing now always resolves by matching a Task's required Skill against eligible Agents, reusing the real `SkillManifest`/eligible-Agent resolution shipped under ADR-104 (TASK-007); ambiguous/no-match escalates to explicit Human assignment. Ownership moved from Internal Strategist to Chief of Staff — its glossary-defined coordinating/routing role and shipped @mention-dispatch precedent. Reschedule-confidence-calibration extended to also calibrate routing; new `routing-approval-gate` Automation reuses the ADR-073 `classifyApprovalBand` system gate.
- Added `target-change-reopen-prompt` Automation and an explicit status invariant: done/archived/parked Tasks accept a governed reverse transition to pending/in_progress on a changed outcome target — the ordinary task-manager "reopen" convention, now uniform since there is only one Task type.
- Confirmed the pre-existing `/task-manager` UI (`platform/apps/web`, live, has a working Status column) and regenerated its data from the merged `docs/TASKS.md`; the already-running dev server on the main checkout serves a separate uncommitted working tree that hasn't pulled the merge, so TASK-021 isn't visible there yet. A second attempt from this worktree hit a pre-existing, unrelated `@bridge/core` build break from the TASK-007 orchestration work — logged to `docs/BUGS.md`, out of scope here.
- Updated docs: `docs/raw/taskmanager-module-plan-2026-07.md`, `docs/raw/brd-taskmanager-2026-07.md`, `docs/wiki/taskmanager.md`, `docs/TASKS.md`, `docs/raw/decisions-log.md` (ADR-106, ADR-107, pointer added to ADR-105), `docs/APPROVALS.md` (AP-035), `docs/requests.md` (R-037), `docs/BUGS.md`, `.claude/launch.json` (added `platform-web-worktree` on port 5175).

# 2026-07-17 — View Grammar / DataEngine BRD: Calendar and Graph confirmed/corrected as View kinds (R-038, AP-036)
- New `docs/raw/brd-dataengine-views-2026-07.md`: canonical 8 View kinds (table/board/gallery/form/calendar/map/graph/tree), each with eligibility rule (computed from column metadata, never hand-assigned), feature list, write path, and worked "which Page renders which views" examples. Full 2026-07-17 code audit of the current state.
- Code audit found real violations of "Calendar is a view, not a Module": `/calendar` route resolves to Task Manager (routing bug), real Google Calendar UI lives at a second route `/calendar/google` behind `InstalledModuleBoundary packageName="calendar"`, and Calendar is catalogued as an installed Module/Tool in `moduleRoutes.ts`/`tools.ts`/`IntelligencePage.tsx`. Four independent, non-shared calendar renderers also found (`DataEngine.tsx`, `CalendarPage.tsx`, `dataviews/CalendarView.tsx`, `InitiativeDetail.tsx`/`WorkPage.tsx` local hardcodes). Target: Calendar becomes purely `kind: "calendar"`, Google Calendar becomes a plain Integration with zero page/route/nav identity of its own. Four rows logged to `docs/BUGS.md`.
- Confirmed correct (no change needed): the relationship graph has no separate entity — Relations render as typed edges over Person/Community rows, gated the same way Calendar is gated (column metadata, not hardcoding). One tracked renderer gap: `GraphView.tsx` is currently a table-with-banner placeholder. Second Brain (cross-Module graph) is explicitly declared a distinct surface from a Page's own Graph view.
- `docs/TASKS.md` TASK-014 (Full standard Module UI rollout) rewritten to explicitly name every deliverable this surfaces: View Grammar consolidation onto the existing `dataviews/registry.ts` system (currently wired only to `/workspace`), Calendar de-modularization, real Graph renderer, `network`→`graph` vocabulary rename (glossary already says "graph"), `tree` kind promotion (introduced by the Task Manager Module).
- ADR-108 records the architectural call. No code changed in this pass — TASK-014 executes the consolidation.
- Durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-17-dataengine-views-brd.md`.

- 2026-07-17 — TASK-010 (platform red-flag correction feedback) implemented, `in_progress`. Human-authored correction Memory writes directly via `memoryStore` (`sourceRefType: "feedback"`, `trustOrigin: "user_content"`, never through the Agent/Skill pipeline); a separate governed `learning.proposePreferenceAdjustment` Skill (new manifest, Goal/Task-resolved per TASK-007's AGS1 contract, `resourceType: "signal"` — never `policy_param`) always drafts a reviewable proposal, never auto-applies. Append-only clear/reopen/updateReason/forget with full audit history. Removed AP-023's green/yellow feedback semantics: deleted the orphaned pre-canon `FlagIcon.tsx` and a dead duplicate `data/jobpilot.ts` fixture; renamed JobPilot's `FlagColor`→`FitRecommendation` (`pursue|review|pass`) and `greenFlags/redFlags`→`strengths/concerns` (DealPilot's equivalent had already been removed by concurrent TASK-006 work). Core 421, DB 107, API 177 (+7 new), JobPilot 93, DealPilot 72, Commons 22, web 56 (+7 new) tests, full monorepo typecheck/build (40/40), changed-file lint, and no-dummy-runtime all pass clean; live HTTP smoke test against a running API server confirmed create/clear/reopen/listAll and fail-closed direct-Human invocation end-to-end. No schema migration needed (TASK-008 RM4's `0015` reservation unaffected). Outstanding: live desktop+375px browser evidence of the actual hover/focus/click interaction. - 2026-07-17 — TASK-010 independent review remediation (round 2), `in_progress`. Commit `e4a2a3c` was found NOT merge-ready: auth/IDOR (used `pilotUserId` instead of `ctx.identity.id`, procedures used the permissive `procedure` not `authenticatedProcedure`), a ledger privacy leak (proposal `inputs` carried raw anchor/renderedValue/reason/rationale, workspace-wide-readable), no saga/idempotency (a retry could duplicate Memory/Task/proposal rows), no lineage concurrency control (stale ids could fork history), weak anchor identity (cell/bullet/file/result targets could collide), no server-side pagination (Settings truncated behind an unrelated 200-row fetch), per-cell N+1 queries, viewport- instead of pointer-based touch detection, and an unbacked persisted-column rename. All 9 items remediated: every `redFlag` procedure is now `authenticatedProcedure` + `assertMembership` with owner always the real caller; the governed proposal's ledger row now carries only `{flagMemoryId, governed, applied, summary}` (no private detail), and `clear`/`forget` withdraw (veto) any still-pending proposal; `create` is a client-`operationId`-driven idempotent two-step saga (Memory write always succeeds/is reused; the governed-learning attempt is separately resumable, recording `learningStatus: "proposed"|"failed"` rather than silently losing a thrown/rejected outcome); `@bridge/core`'s `MemoryStore` gained a real `casSupersede`/`currentForLineage` atomic primitive (in-memory: synchronous check-and-mutate mirroring `InMemoryLedger`; persistent: a genuine Postgres SERIALIZABLE transaction, verified against real concurrent pglite transactions, not simulated); `RedFlagAnchor` became a discriminated `cell`/`bullet` union with a NUL-joined canonical string hashed into a deterministic UUID lineage key (reusing the existing indexed `subject_element_id` column — no new migration); `listAll`/`history` gained cursor pagination and full lineage history; a new `RedFlagProvider` React context batches one query per table/record instead of one per cell; touch/pointer handling now uses `[@media(hover:none)]`/`[@media(pointer:coarse)]` with a real >=44px hit target. JobPilot's flag-value rename got a documented `normalizeLegacyFitFlag` interim safety net — the actual backfill/CHECK-constraint migration is explicitly left PENDING (not fabricated) until TASK-008 RM4's `0015` lands, with the exact plan recorded. Re-verified: core 426, DB 111, API 187, JobPilot 94, DealPilot 72, Commons 22, integrations-google 35, web 57 tests, full monorepo typecheck/build (40/40), lint, and no-dummy-runtime all pass clean, 0 regressions. Outstanding: live desktop+375px browser evidence, the pending RM4-dependent migration, and a fresh independent review of this remediated commit. Durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-17-task010-review-remediation.md`.

- 2026-07-17 — TASK-010 fresh independent review of the round-2 remediation found 2 further real bugs, both fixed same day. (a) `redFlag.create`'s idempotent-retry check read the immutable step-1 Memory's own frozen `learningStatus: "none"` instead of re-resolving the lineage's actual current state — a genuine retry silently re-invoked `pipeline.propose` a second time with the same deterministic `proposalId` (masked only by an unrelated CAS fallback that happened to converge the client-visible result); fixed by resolving `currentForLineage(workspaceId, ownerId, anchorKey)` before deciding whether to skip the governed-learning step, with a new test asserting `pipeline.propose` is invoked exactly once across a create-then-retry pair. (b) `DrizzleMemoryStore`'s `isSerializationFailure` checked `err.code` at the top level, but drizzle-orm >=0.45 wraps every driver error in `DrizzleQueryError` with the real Postgres error (carrying `.code`) on `.cause` — the exact wrapping `ledger-store.ts`'s `isRefLedgerUniqueViolation` already works around; a genuine Postgres `40001` serialization failure would have re-thrown as an unhandled `DrizzleQueryError` instead of gracefully returning a CAS-failure `null`, defeating item 4's whole concurrency guarantee under real contention — undetected by the original concurrency test because pglite's single-connection execution model didn't happen to produce a real write-skew race. Fixed by walking the `.cause` chain identically to the ledger precedent; added a direct unit test constructing a real `DrizzleQueryError`-wrapped `40001` (verifying the unwrap at multiple nesting depths and that unrelated SQLSTATEs are correctly rejected) plus a `casSupersede`-integration test via a monkeypatched `db.transaction` proving the method returns `null` rather than re-throwing. Re-verified after both fixes: core 426, DB 113 (+2 new), API 187, JobPilot 94, DealPilot 72, Commons 22, integrations-google 35, web 57 tests, full monorepo typecheck/build (40/40), lint, and no-dummy-runtime all pass clean, 0 regressions. Outstanding: live desktop+375px browser evidence and the pending RM4-dependent migration (item 9). Durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-17-task010-review-remediation.md`.

- 2026-07-17 — TASK-010 round-4 remediation: coordinator's final review found 11 remaining defects across governed-learning substance, private-proposal ownership, saga replay-safety, clear/reopen/forget durability, canonical anchor validation, DB-side filtering, keyset pagination, reason-save serialization, hybrid touch, and JobPilot legacy-flag normalization. All 11 fixed: (1) `create` now synthesizes a real, structured, owner-private `preference_adjustment` Memory (scope/target/proposed-change/rationale/evidence-ref, resolved under owner-authorized read) instead of an opaque echo; a SEPARATE `redFlag.enactCorrection`/`revokeCorrection` pair (Human-authorized, never Agent/pipeline.propose) applies/undoes the actual correction (a visible display suppression) ONLY after ledger approval, and clear/forget/an explicit revoke all permanently block re-enactment. (2) `action.listPending`/`decide` now filter/reject a `visibility:"private"` proposal for every non-owner member while leaving ordinary team proposals' visibility/decidability completely unchanged. (3) `create`'s saga reconciles against the ledger's own existing entry BEFORE re-proposing (a crash between ledger-append and the outcome CAS now recovers to "proposed," never misreported as "failed"), and a genuinely failed attempt is retryable through a later `create` call (previously stuck forever). (4) `clear` now withdraws/revokes BEFORE flipping status (a withdrawal failure leaves the flag completely untouched, safely retryable) and sets an accurate `learningStatus`; `reopen` mints a FRESH governed proposal for the newly-reopened version rather than ever resurrecting a permanently-resolved one. (5) `forget` enumerates every version across the WHOLE lineage (not just whichever id the caller passed) and withdraws every distinct linked proposal before deleting. (6) `redFlag.create` validates a cell/bullet's record-shaped target server-side against its owning module's real store (JobPilot/DealPilot-initiative/Touchpoint/Person/Community existence + workspace ownership) — an unrecognized module fails closed; `TableView.tsx` no longer falls back to a sorted row's array index as a flaggable anchor's `recordId`. (7) `@bridge/core`'s `MemoryQuery` gained a `contentPathEquals` JSON-predicate mechanism (Postgres `content::jsonb #>>`, no migration) pushing `listForScope`'s dominant `anchor.moduleId`/`kind` reducers into the DB query. (8) `MemoryQuery` also gained real keyset `order`/`cursor` pagination (both adapters); `listAll`/`history` now use a genuine `(createdAt,id)` cursor instead of a mutable offset, and `history`'s prior 200-version cap is gone. (9) `RedFlagProvider`'s mutations now apply their OWN returned row to the local cache synchronously (never solely awaiting a background refetch), and `RedFlagControl`'s reason-save resolves to the flagId the SAVE itself produced — a following clear/reopen/forget click can no longer race a stale pre-save id. (10) touch/pointer CSS now also gates on `(any-pointer: coarse)` alongside `(pointer: coarse)`/`(hover: none)`, covering a touch digitizer that coexists with a non-touch primary pointer. (11) JobPilot's legacy `green`/`yellow`/`red` flag values are now normalized at `DrizzleJobPilotStore`'s own read boundary (`createJob`/`listJobs`/`updateApplication`/`getApplication`), not only a test-only helper — no legacy value can reach a client through any current call path. While implementing, 3 further real bugs were found and fixed by executing (not just reading) the code: a stale `.get(id)` read of the preference-adjustment lineage after `enactCorrection` had already superseded it (fixed to `currentForLineage`); a non-monotonic, seed-derived outcome-Memory id that collided on the SAME primary key on any retry whose learning status genuinely changed (fixed to a fresh `uuidv7()` per attempt, mirroring clear/reopen/updateReason's own pattern); and every superseding red-flag write silently inheriting its PREDECESSOR's `createdAt` via an unguarded `{...current}` spread, which broke keyset/history ordering once two writes landed in the same millisecond (fixed via a process-local monotonic timestamp helper applied to all 10 red-flag write sites). Re-verified: core 426, DB 118 (+5 new: MemoryQuery keyset/predicate tests, jobpilot-store legacy-flag test), API 203 (33 red-flag tests, up from 17), JobPilot 94, DealPilot 72, Commons 22, integrations-google 35, web 59 (10 red-flag-control tests) tests, full monorepo build (23/23 packages, zero errors), typecheck clean, lint clean (0 new findings), no-dummy-runtime clean. A pre-existing, unrelated dummy-data instance discovered along the way (`JobPilotApplicationDetail.tsx`'s unrouted `BCG_APPLICATION` fixture, predating this task) was logged to `docs/dummy.md` rather than silently left untracked. Item 9/11's JobPilot flag-column migration remains explicitly PENDING (TASK-008 RM4 owns migration 0015, not yet landed). Outstanding: live desktop+375px browser evidence and the pending RM4-dependent migration. Durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-17-task010-round4-remediation.md`.

- 2026-07-17 — TASK-010 round-4 fresh independent review found ONE real gap in the just-pushed commit: `action.resolution` was missing the `isProposalVisibleTo` private-proposal ownership check that `action.listPending`/`decide` already had, letting any workspace member observe a private red-flag proposal's resolution state and eventual approve/veto decision by proposalId. Fixed same day: `resolution` now rejects `NOT_FOUND` for a private proposal the caller doesn't own (never confirming existence to a non-owner, mirroring `MemoryStore.get`'s convention); added pre- and post-decision assertions to the `PRIVATE PROPOSALS` test. Re-verified: api 203/203 (34 red-flag tests), lint clean. All other 13 of 14 reviewed claims (11 items + 3 self-found bugs) were independently confirmed by execution, including genuine break/restore tests on items 4, 5, 7, and all 3 bugs. Durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-17-task010-round4-remediation.md` (updated).

- 2026-07-17 — TASK-010 round-5 remediation: coordinator review of `b177778` found 11 more defects, 2 CRITICAL (legacy-API Memory-kind leakage; DB RLS bypass). All 11 addressed. (1) `onboarding.learningState`/`forgetMemory` were unauthenticated/`pilotUserId`-scoped with no kind filter — any caller could read/delete private `red_flag`/`preference_adjustment` content; now `authenticatedProcedure` + `assertMembership`, owner-scoped to `ctx.identity.id`, kind-whitelisted (`learningState`) or kind-rejecting (`forgetMemory`, forces deletion through `redFlag.forget`). (2) Owner-aware DB RLS: investigated and documented rather than fabricated — `ledger`'s workspace-wide RLS lets a direct-Supabase client see a private proposal's existence/opaque-summary (bounded, not raw content, thanks to round 4's opaque payload); the one offending client read (`data/ledger.ts`'s `loadLedger()`) and the exact two-part fix (RLS migration + a new paginated ledger-listing tRPC endpoint, neither of which exists yet) logged to `docs/BUGS.md`, blocked on RM4. (3) `RedFlagControl.tsx` now checks `action.resolution` on-demand and exposes "Enact correction," closing the gap where an approved correction had no UI trigger. (4) A thrown (not merely rejected) governed-learning attempt no longer persists an unconfirmed `proposalId`; `withdrawPendingRedFlagProposal` treats a confirmed-absent ledger entry as already-withdrawn instead of throwing the ledger's generic "no entry" error; added a `retryLearning` mutation so a `learningStatus:"failed"` flag can be retried directly (no forced Clear-then-Reopen). (5) Audited every listed saga race; added a genuine concurrent `Promise.allSettled` test for `updateReason`-vs-`clear` on the same flag (exactly one wins, lineage never forks) — the rest were already covered by rounds 2-4's CAS/reconciliation pattern; the durable DB-outbox table itself is migration-gated. (6) A single shared `canonicalModuleId()` normalizer now feeds BOTH anchor hashing and server-side validation (an alias can never fork a lineage — new test proves `"job-pilot"`/`"jobpilot"` collide on the SAME flag); `JobPilotApplicationDetail.tsx` (unrouted, fixture-only) had its red-flag wiring removed entirely; `TableView.tsx` gates on a new client-side supported-module allowlist excluding `"signal"` (no existence-check store yet); `JobPilotPage.tsx`'s Card view now ships the required REAL, persisted-application-backed rendered-bullet surface (Stage/Fit bullets, closing the "JobPilot's strengths/concerns are never persisted" gap from rounds 4-5). (7) The process-local monotonic-timestamp lineage-ordering limitation (invalid cross-instance/restart) is now explicitly documented with the planned DB-backed revision-column fix, blocked on RM4. (8) Fixed a confirmed-reproducible `content::jsonb` cast failure in `retrieve()`'s `contentPathEquals` (Postgres doesn't guarantee AND-clause evaluation order — a single non-JSON row anywhere in the matching set could break the query) via a SQL-standard-guaranteed `CASE WHEN content IS JSON` construct. (9) `redFlag.listAll`'s `status` filter is now pushed into the store query alongside `kind`, not applied app-side post-page. (10) Fixed two genuine concurrency bugs: `provisionRedFlagLearningTask`'s Goal provisioning was a check-then-act race with a non-deterministic id (now deterministic + getGoal-first + catch-and-recheck, mirroring the existing Task-creation pattern); `casSupersede` only caught `40001` (serialization failure), not `23505` (unique violation) — a deterministic Memory id means two real concurrent callers can both pass the "no current row" compare and then collide on the SAME insert, which is a DIFFERENT Postgres failure mode; added `isMemoryIdUniqueViolation` (scoped to `memories_pkey`) and confirmed via a genuine reproduced race (not just a constructed-error test) that it now resolves to CAS-failure `null` instead of an unhandled throw. (11) `RedFlagProvider` now distinguishes loading/error/empty (a failed refresh preserves last-good rows instead of clearing them to `[]`), guards overlapping refreshes with a generation counter, and disables `create()` until the first successful load (surfaced inline in `RedFlagControl` via `role="alert"`, no unhandled rejection). Re-verified: core 426, DB 121 (+3 new), API 211 (+21 across rounds), JobPilot 94, DealPilot 72, Commons 22, integrations-google 35, web 64 (+7 new) tests; the two new concurrency tests (Goal-provisioning race, updateReason-vs-clear race) each re-run 3x with no flakes. Full monorepo `turbo run typecheck` (37/37) and `build` (20/20) clean; a full serialized `test --concurrency=1 --continue` run is 36/37 green (the one failure, `@bridge/sensors`'s coverage floor, is a pre-existing, already-documented, unrelated baseline gap — not this session's code); lint clean on every session-touched file (2 pre-existing unrelated repo-wide findings untouched); no-dummy-runtime clean. Items 2 (RLS) and 7 (durable lineage revision) remain genuinely open pending TASK-008 RM4's migration `0015`. `docs/dummy.md`'s existing `BCG_APPLICATION` fixture entry updated to note its red-flag wiring removal. Outstanding: live desktop+375px browser evidence and the pending RM4-dependent migration/RLS work. Durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-17-task010-round5-remediation.md`.

- 2026-07-17 — TASK-010 round-6: fresh independent review of round-5 (`a7a33b9`) traced every claimed fix against the actual code (not the commit message) and re-ran every affected test suite independently. Verdict: merge-ready — all 11 round-5 items VERIFIED-FIXED. Two non-blocking follow-ups fixed same day: `docs/BUGS.md`'s RLS entry overstated the leak as including the proposal's `onBehalfOf` identity (`LEDGER_COLS` doesn't select `on_behalf_of_id`, and a red-flag proposal's opaque `inputs` carries no `display.onBehalfOf` — corrected to the accurate, narrower leak surface); and `RedFlagProvider`'s `error` state had no UI consumer (a failed initial load was a permanent dead end with a stuck "loading" label) — fixed with a `retryLoad()` context method and a click-to-retry affordance on the flag glyph specifically when `ctx.loading && ctx.error`. - Reviewed all 178 candidates from the earlier Parallel.ai FindAll run (`findall_f1276700e2d243b4a9069467727fd8ce`) for relevance to the Learning Agent's LA3 web-research/recon capability — not just the 46 the run originally matched against "Parallel.ai competitor" conditions. # 2026-07-18 — TASK-022 inference cost optimization implemented
- Replaced model registration-order selection with explicit cheap/default/reasoning tier routing while preserving the Local-Plane-never-falls-to-cloud invariant.
- Added Anthropic stable-system-prefix caching, normalized provider usage/model metadata, dated cost estimates where pricing is known, and an attributable prompt-free Chief-of-Staff receipt in the append-only ledger.
- Added deterministic provider-protocol, cache-threshold, usage, tier-selection, governed-receipt, stable-prefix, and local fail-closed regressions. ADR-140; durable outcome: `outputs/2026-07-18-task-022-inference-cost-optimization.md`.
- Independent correctness/security review findings were resolved before commit: membership now precedes model egress; receipts use UUID-compatible attributable principals; model/accounting errors fail closed; Anthropic null versus omitted usage matches the official protocol; and only the accurately priced five-minute cache TTL is exposed.
- Final security re-review found membership alone still bypassed Authority/Plane/policy for configured providers. One governed wrapper now authorizes cloud calls as public-scope Egress-Agent fetches on behalf of the member (local calls stay local principal reads), blocks before provider access, and records policy-bearing receipts for classification, Communications, and foundational-Agent responses.
- Final blast-radius hardening rejects tier-mismatched completions and non-finite cost estimates, bounds receipt identifiers, and surfaces provider HTTP status without retaining arbitrary response bodies.
- 2026-07-21 recovery addendum: preserved the paused work as `85474fa`, normally merged `origin/main@2325cc5`, and resolved nine code/docs conflicts by composition without rebase, stash, reset, force, or vocabulary rollback. Current Organization/Module/Commons/Task contracts win; the colliding branch-local ADR-113 became ADR-140.
- Landing review proved arbitrary CoS text could still be sent to cloud while being labeled public. Fixed at the root: CoS now defaults Local Plane, cloud requires an authenticated per-turn public-data declaration plus explicit confirmation, policy checks the confirmation, and the receipt stores no prompt. Correctness re-review and separate bounded security review found no remaining issue.
- Final affected gates: core 59, models 27, API 13 passed; one secure live Anthropic test skipped; three package builds and typechecks, 23-file ESLint, 12 vocabulary tests/zero-term ratchet, no-dummy, and diff integrity passed. AP-067 moves TASK-022 `ready`→`blocked` on the exact absent authorized Anthropic credential/live-spend/cache-read evidence gate; no provider endpoint or billing path was exercised.

# 2026-07-18 — TASK-006 DealPilot durable Local Plane and explicit OS vault

- Replaced process-local DealPilot Records, Relations, captures, candidate profiles, Gmail
  continuation/checkpoint/receipt state, settlements, spend, dedupe, and credential audit with one
  atomic Organization-scoped Local Plane aggregate.
- Added shared-client PGlite state, canonical-path cross-process ownership, restart recovery,
  idempotent materialization/backfill, startup cleanup, and desktop parent-loss/shutdown handling.
- Added pre-Drizzle legacy adapter-table detection plus verified, resumable import into the
  adapter-owned namespace, avoiding a numbered migration.
- Added maintained MIT `@napi-rs/keyring` behind the Source credential-vault port with unique
  per-write accounts, opaque Organization/Source-bound references, masked projections, and
  fail-closed runtime configuration.
- Added opaque crash-recovery journals for keyring create/revoke; values never enter Bridge
  database, files, logs, audit, or the journal.
- Added restart, concurrency, compensation, ownership, recovery, and credential non-disclosure
  coverage. No numbered migration was added; RM4 `0015` and TASK-010 sequencing remain untouched.
- Post-RM4 reconciliation retained its durable decision/effect ledger by binding file-backed
  in-memory-mode ledgers to the same `localDir`; added password-AMR-only re-authentication,
  single-current-session replacement, credential log redaction, bounded capture pagination, safe
  clipboard clearing, and removal of sampled provider payloads from discovery output.
- Added owner-scoped explicit credential revoke: secure-vault deletion, durable Source-projection
  clearing across restart, value-free audit, re-auth session consumption, and honest unavailable UI.
- Independent security review found the durable desktop sidecar trusted loopback. Fixed with a
  per-launch 256-bit capability, constant-time API verification, closed Tauri-origin CORS,
  file-backed-persistence auth posture, header redaction, and a hard separation from Human re-auth.
- Follow-up review found predictable Google OAuth state could substitute an attacker's account.
  Connect now issues a hashed-at-rest 256-bit state after authenticated membership; callback
  atomically consumes the ten-minute state and Local Plane PKCE verifier before denial handling or
  verifier-bound code exchange.
- Final review pass found privileged-webview OAuth navigation could expose the launch capability.
  Release webviews now reject external top-level navigation, token injection is trusted-origin and
  main-only, and validated Google consent opens in the system browser.
- Final central review then found packaged desktop Google calls bypassed injected sidecar transport,
  the launcher set ignored `HOST` instead of `API_HOST`, and OAuth completion trusted stale
  membership (including revocation during provider exchange). Shared transport headers, enforced
  loopback, authenticated cross-platform shutdown, and pre/post-exchange membership checks close
  those paths. Focused correctness/security re-review: no findings.
- Later review hardening made all token operations per-Integration serialized, kept exchanged tokens
  provisional until membership finalization with exact rollback, and made refresh persistence
  ordered CAS. Rust now binds and retains the random loopback listener while Node inherits the same
  descriptor; child death cannot donate the port. Active requests drain under an independent hard
  deadline, and unsupported non-Unix release activation fails closed.
- Live release validation found two lifecycle gaps: missing Tauri data-URL support prevented the
  tokenless bootstrap, and uniform webview destruction aborted on a macOS `AvatarPanel`. The feature
  is explicit, the bootstrap handle is retained and hidden before destruction, and sidecar-loss
  cleanup uses panel-safe conversion. Simulated child death left the shell alive, displayed the
  unavailable state, and kept the port unrebindable.
- Final read-only security review found no vulnerabilities. Correctness review raised only releasing
  ownership after a failed client close; the explicit retention regression remains because the
  embedded client may still be live and admitting a second opener risks corruption.
- Final post-merge verification: API 239, DealPilot 90, Local Plane 8, DB 155, Core 430, Sourcing 7,
  Company Sourcing 4, Google 39, Web 70, and desktop Rust 44 tests; monorepo 37-task typecheck and
  20-task build, changed-file lint, Clippy, no-runtime-dummy, and diff checks passed.
- Normally merged `origin/main` `bab32ea` after preserving the implementation. Relationship
  continuity and its landed `0016` migration remain intact; TASK-006 adds no migration delta.
  Canon collisions were reconciled as AP-044 and ADR-117–ADR-120, and hardened PKCE/token
  finalization was retained through the OAuth conflict.
- Live host evidence: macOS keyring write/read/delete/missing; native DealPilot route plus final
  release-sidecar readiness/failure recovery; Chrome `/dealpilot/sources` at 375x812 with no
  horizontal overflow.
- TASK-006 remains `in_progress` for live Google/BizBuySell and verified OS/application re-auth.
  Signing and physical-mobile certification remain unclaimed. Durable outcome:
  `outputs/2026-07-18-task-006-dealpilot-local-durability.md`.

(2) MEDIUM privacy — `compareAndSupersede`'s artifact-purge pattern only redacted the current row, leaving raw fetched-artifact bytes readable forever in the superseded ancestor row; fixed with a new `MemoryStore.redactLineageContent` (in-memory + Drizzle parity, restart-durability proof).
- Merged `origin/main` (`5ca30ca`, TASK-010's red-flag correction feature + TASK-003's drag fix) forward into the TASK-011 branch. Reconciled real conflicts in 8 files (`memory-store.ts` core+db, `wiring.ts`, `router.ts`, `governance-stores.ts`, `local-store.test.ts`, `memory-store.test.ts`, `pending-work.generated.json`) where both branches independently modified the same regions — no logic dropped from either side (confirmed by a fresh independent review). Fixed a browser-build break the merge's item-1 fix introduced (`node:crypto` import bundled into the web app) with a dependency-free FNV-1a hash, and 3 genuine test-vs-implementation mismatches surfaced by TASK-010's `touchpoint`→`event` rename and its client-plane hardening.
- Verified: full monorepo build (21/21) clean; `@bridge/core` 448/448; `@bridge/db` 165/165; `@bridge/jobpilot` 125/125; `@bridge/net-guard` 24/24; `@bridge/api` 300/300 (low-load full-suite run; heavy-load reruns reproduced the same pre-existing real-socket-timing flakes documented in every prior round of this task); no schema drift; no-dummy-runtime clean; lint's 2 findings confirmed pre-existing on a clean `origin/main` checkout.
